计算值（均值、标准差、DID、delta）独立重算确认\n- [ ] 所有 evaluation protocol 差异已在论文中说明\n- [ ] 多 seed 结果报告均值和标准差\n- [ ] 单 seed 数据在 Limitations 中声明\n\n### 5.4 图表检查\n\n- [ ] 所有 figure PDF 存在且路径匹配\n- [ ] 图表不超栏宽（单栏 5.5in / 全宽 6.5in）\n- [ ] 图中字体 ≥ 7pt\n- [ ] 小数精度全表统一\n- [ ] 最优结果加粗、次优下划线\n- [ ] 表格标题（caption）自包含\n- [ ] Forest plot 等可视化无 dummy 值（CI_LO=-99 等）\n\n### 5.5 引用检查\n\n- [ ] 引用覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**（任一缺失说明调研不充分）\n- [ ] 引用密度与目标会议近年录用论文同量级\n- [ ] 引用键与 bib 条目 1:1 匹配\n- [ ] 无孤儿 bib 条目\n- [ ] 无幻觉引用（所有被引论文实际存在）\n- [ ] 近年论文的年份/会议准确\n- [ ] 审稿人要求的引用全部补齐\n\n### 5.6 匿名化检查\n\n- [ ] 源代码无硬编码绝对路径（`/Users/...`, `/root/...`）\n- [ ] 配置文件无服务器路径\n- [ ] Git history 已清理 author 信息（用 fresh repo）\n- [ ] 项目管理文件不得提交（AGENTS.md, memory.md, registry.yaml, task_context/ 等）\n- [ ] API Keys 通过环境变量，无泄露\n\n### 5.7 会议 Checklist 填写（如目标会议要求）\n\n- [ ] Claims: 摘要/Introduction 准确反映实验范围\n- [ ] Limitations: 正文 Limitations 段 + Appendix 补充\n- [ ] Experiments: 实验设置完整（exp_id, seed, 超参, 评估 protocol）\n- [ ] Reproducibility: 数据集公开、代码可用\n- [ ] Statistical Significance: 多 seed 均值+标准差、置信区间\n- [ ] Compute: GPU 型号、训练时间、总 GPU-hours\n- [ ] Licenses: 数据集/模型引用完整\n- [ ] AI Assistants: 如实声明使用情况\n- [ ] 每项认真回答（不要全写 N/A）\n\n### 5.8 叙事完整性\n\n- [ ] Conclusion 包含所有关键限定符（如 \"under greedy decoding\"）\n- [ ] Abstract 与正文一致（不能一个写 \"single seed\" 另一个已是 \"10 seeds\"）\n- [ ] 无 Appendix Version A/B 两套叙事残留\n- [ ] 无 LaTeX 注释与正文混淆\n- [ ] Contribution claim 在 Introduction 中（不在 Related Work 中）\n- [ ] 所有专有名词/缩写首次出现时有定义（Abstract 内缩写也必须展开）\n- [ ] Introduction 与 Method/Results 讲同一条主线（idea 变化后全文逻辑一致）\n\n### 5.9 去 AI 风格检查\n\n- [ ] **`grep \"—\" paper/*.tex` 全文清零**（解释性 em-dash 必须全删，最显著的 AI 痕迹）\n- [ ] **`grep \" – \" paper/*.tex` 全文清零**（解释性 en-dash 必须全删）\n- [ ] 无 \"-ing,\" 分词短语结尾（如 \"..., demonstrating the effectiveness of...\"）\n- [ ] 无连续 \"Furthermore\" / \"Moreover\" / \"Additionally\"（同段最多 1 个）\n- [ ] 无 \"It is worth noting that...\" / \"Notably, ...\"\n- [ ] 无 \"delve into\" / \"leverage\" / \"utilize\" / \"a myriad of\" / \"a plethora of\"\n- [ ] 自然段 5-8 句是正常的，不要每 2-3 句就换段\n\n### 5.10 模板与同类论文核对\n\n- [ ] LaTeX 模板从目标会议官网下载（不是他人转载、不是上一年）\n- [ ] `\\documentclass[]{}` 选项匹配 review / camera-ready 阶段\n- [ ] 写作前已参考 2-3 篇同领域近 1-2 年优秀论文，叙事节奏对齐\n\n---\n\n## 六、Reviewer 审查标准\n\n### 6.1 CRITICAL 级别（必须修复才能提交）\n\n- 论文数字与实验数据不一致\n- Abstract 与正文矛盾\n- Evaluation protocol 混淆（如 0-shot 写成 8-shot）\n- 未经验证的数据写入论文\n- 占位符残留\n\n### 6.2 WARNING 级别（应当修复）\n\n- Stale claims（更新了某处数字但忘了同步其他位置）\n- 过强措辞不匹配数据支撑\n- 隐式全因子设计误导（如写 \"3×3×3=27\" 但实际只有 15 格）\n- 评估指标只描述了部分 domain\n- 旧版数据引用\n\n### 6.3 数据一致性审查流程\n\n1. 从 registry 提取所有实验 metrics\n2. 对照论文中每一个内联数字（到行号级别）\n3. 独立重算所有衍生值\n4. 不一致处标注为 CRITICAL\n5. 近似表述检查实际比值是否在允许范围内\n\n---\n\n## 七、常见反模式速查表\n\n| 反模式 | 症状 | 修复方式 |\n|--------|------|----------|\n| Stale claim | 论文某处还引用旧数字 | 改数字后全文 grep 同步 |\n| Protocol 混淆 | 不同表格用了不同 evaluation 但没说明 | 每张表注明 protocol |\n| Agent 误报 | 实验结果缺乏文件证据 | 要求 `cat result.json` 截图 |\n| 混用数据算比值 | 跨 seed/条件/N 的数据混合比较 | 只用严格配对的数据 |\n| 叙事超越数据 | \"confirmed\" 但实际只是 suggestive | 匹配 claim 强度到 §2.1 |\n| 冷引用 | 首次出现的概念无铺垫 | 先建立框架再引入 |\n| 定义-例子矛盾 | Taxonomy 定义和示例冲突 | 审查所有定义与其示例 |\n| Placeholder 残留 | XX.XX, TODO, [If...] 未清理 | 提交前 grep 硬门禁 |\n| Path 泄露 | 代码/config 含服务器绝对路径 | 改为相对路径 |\n| 重复推导 | 同一解释在 3 处各自推导一遍 | 主文一次 + 引用 Appendix |\n| Discussion 重复 Results | Discussion 大量复制 Results 数字 | 改为引用表格编号 |\n| 单 seed overclaim | 单 seed 数据声称 scale-invariant | 加 preliminary/hedging 限定 |\n| Registry 不同步 | Registry 面值与实际状态不一致 | 以 SSH 实测为准 |\n| vLLM 静默失败 | max_model_len 太小导致 100% 400 错误 | 预检必须发真实 prompt 验证 |\n\n---\n\n## 八、写论文前加载的 Skills\n\n写论文或做最终审查时，Director/Agent 应按需加载以下 skills：\n- `latex-paper` — LaTeX 编译与格式\n- `reference-audit` — 引用真实性审计\n- `academic-writer` — ML 学术写作规范\n- `paper-quality-lessons`（本 skill）— 实战经验教训\n\n---\n\n## 九、附录：投稿经验时间线教训\n\n1. **论文数字锁定时机**：最终编译前 24h 锁定所有数字，之后只允许格式修改\n2. **GPU 不要空闲**：审稿期间如果 Agent 空闲，应该跑 ablation/扩展实验作为 rebuttal 储备\n3. **匿名化提前做**：不要等到最后一天才检查路径泄露，fresh repo 需要时间清理\n4. **9 页零余量策略**：NeurIPS 正文 9 页是硬限，每次新增内容必须等量压缩并编译验证\n5. **Contingency 叙事提前写**：关键实验完成前就写好两版叙事，避免最后一刻仓促改稿\n",
````json
      "numLines": 301,

    }
````

```text
Now I have a complete picture. Let me compile my full review findings and send to the Director.

Let me organize my findings:

## Part 1: Post-edit consistency check

### Finding 1: Abstract L7 vs Intro C4 — CONSISTENT ✓
Abstract L7: "across two random seeds"
C4: "robust to the EMA smoothing parameter and random seed"
Minor difference in specificity (Abstract more precise with "two"), but no contradiction.

### Finding 2: Conclusion numbers repeat Experiments numbers — INFO
Conclusion L6 repeats exact numbers from Experiments (max Δ_t values). 

The style guide notes that Discussion sections shouldn't repeat specific numbers from Results, but since this paper lacks a separate Discussion and the Conclusion fills that role, some repetition is acceptable and fairly standard. This is a low-priority issue.

### Finding 3: Conclusion L14 "linear and predictable" — WARNING
Claims PPL degradation is "linear" at fixed r, but only Gen 10 endpoints are shown in Table 3. No intermediate PPL trajectory for r ≥ 0.3 is provided. "Linear" is a specific mathematical claim without supporting evidence.

### Finding 4: Conclusion L3
The claim that SLV "captures volume changes before they manifest as dimensionality reduction" only holds at r=0.2 (Experiments L137). Under pure collapse (r=0), SLV and r_eff peak simultaneously, so the "before" framing is regime-dependent rather than universal. The statement itself is technically about SLV's capability rather than making a temporal claim, though.

### Finding 5: Limitations §3.1 anti-pattern — WARNING
The Limitations section is a dense 17-line block that should be split into 2-3 thematic subsections according to the style guide. Currently five distinct limitation topics are woven together in one flowing paragraph and need clearer structural separation.

### Finding 6: Em-dash status — PASS ✓
No Unicode em-dashes found.

### Finding 7: Abstract/Intro terminology consistency — CONSISTENT ✓
"Signal attenuation paradox" and "three-phase pattern/trajectory" are used consistently throughout, and "spectral log-volume (SLV)" is properly introduced.

### Finding 8: Related Work SIGMA comparison self-consistency — CONSISTENT ✓
The speedup multipliers (3--8×) align with the Pythia model results, the recursive generation count matches, and the "premature conclusion" argument is consistent with Phase I findings.

### Finding 9: Method Eq.1 clip vs Experiments — CONSISTENT ✓
The clipping operation in Eq.1 corresponds to the [0.20, 0.80] bounds in Experiments, and the controller parameters are consistent across both sections.

### Finding 10: Redundancy between Conclusion and Related Work — INFO
Both sections use nearly identical phrasing about the failure mode not being previously characterized in the EWS literature, which is redundant though not contradictory.

### Finding 11: Conclusion L14 linearity claim vs Table 3 — ISSUE
The conclusion claims quality degradation is linear and predictable with a Table 3 reference, but Table 3 only shows the final generation endpoint, not the full trajectory needed to support a linearity claim.

## Part 2: Discussion/Conclusion structure audit

Looking at how the conclusion is organized: it opens with the three-phase summary and monitoring approach, then moves into the phase diagram and signal attenuation details, followed by the practical r_crit alternative and workflow guidance. After that it discusses the EWS framework extension, web contamination, and four future directions with a validation caveat. The flow from summary to detail works, but I need to check whether the transitions between these major sections are logical and whether each paragraph builds appropriately on what came before.

The progression from practical workaround back to theoretical contribution creates a slight logic jump—it would be cleaner to group the theoretical contributions together before returning to practice. That said, prioritizing the practical takeaway for an applied audience is defensible. The transition from signal attenuation into r_crit is clean, but there's no explicit bridge between the practitioner workflow and the theoretical framework extension that follows. The rest of the flow—from theory into applied extensions and then future work—holds together well.

I'm also noting that Table 1 needs updating once the remaining experiments finish: the r=0 row currently shows a two-seed mean but should reflect three seeds, and the caption needs to be adjusted accordingly.

For the r=0.25 condition in lines 139-142, I need to replace the single-seed trajectory with a two-seed version that includes the cross-seed mean. Table 3 will need similar updates—r=0 gets the three-seed mean while r=0.25 gets the two-seed mean, and the caption at line 182 requires revision.

In the Conclusion, the maximum Δ_t value at r=0.25 might shift once I compute the cross-seed mean, though the r_crit bound is unlikely to change. The Limitations section just needs the seed counts updated to reflect that the third seed is now complete and r=0.25 has moved from single-seed to two-seed data.

Now I'm identifying sections that can be polished before the data updates are finalized—adding a transitional sentence between paragraphs 3 and 4 in the Conclusion, restructuring the Limitations into distinct named subsections, and reconsidering the phrasing around "linear and predictable" at line 14 to either soften it or tie it more directly to supporting evidence.
```

距 May 25 DDL 还有 4 天。exp_013 今晚完成后 cuda:1 会释放（HellaSwag eval ~2.5h 后空闲），exp_014 明天完成后 cuda:0 也释放。

- Cross-scale: Pythia-410M full 10 gen + Pythia-1B pilot 3 gen
- Controller: adaptive k=10 vs fixed_mix at r=0.5 (10 gen each)
- Alternative EWS: Table 2 对比 6 个指标

请评估：

1. **实验矩阵有没有对论文评分影响大的 gap？** 哪些补充实验能最显著提升审稿人印象？例如：

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The Director is asking me to evaluate the experiment matrix completeness and recommend whether to run additional experiments vs. focusing on paper polish, with 4 days until the May 25 DDL.

Let me think about this carefully from the perspective of maximizing EMNLP 2026 Overall Assessment.

First, let me understand the current state:
- 5 mixing ratios tested: r ∈ {0, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.5}


- Multi-seed coverage is uneven across ratios, with r=0 having the most runs at 3 seeds while r=0.2 and r=0.5 only have 1 seed each
- I've tested across two model scales (410M and 1B) with 3 generation variants, plus compared adaptive versus fixed control strategies
- I've also evaluated 7 different early warning signal indicators

Now I'm thinking about what a reviewer would likely critique. The phase transition boundary between r=0.25 and r=0.3 is still somewhat coarse-grained, and the seed coverage is quite sparse for some mixing ratios.

However, the paper isn't claiming to pinpoint the exact critical ratio—it's characterizing an interval, which is appropriate for this type of analysis. The single-seed runs at r=0.2 and r=0.5 aren't problematic since they're far from the boundary conditions; the critical region itself (r=0.25 and r=0.3) has adequate replication. For the controller ablation, I've only tested k=10, but the core argument is about a fundamental structural limitation that affects any choice of k, not about optimizing the parameter itself.

The scale limitation with 410M and 1B models is real but already acknowledged. Looking at potential additional experiments, testing r=0.1 or r=0.15 would only add more Type II points below the critical region without tightening any claimed boundary, so that's low value. Similarly, a second seed at r=0.5 would just confirm what's already expected deep in Type III territory—the real insight lies in the 0.25-0.3 transition zone.

A second seed at r=0.2 is more interesting since it's the other Type II condition and currently only tested once. It would validate reproducibility and reveal cross-seed variance for that critical max Δ_t = 0.0019 value sitting just below τ=0.002, which anchors the signal attenuation argument. But there's a risk here—if a second seed shows max Δ_t exceeding 0.002, it would actually undermine the core claim that controller signals stay suppressed even at r=0.2.

For the controller ablation on k and τ, the paper's argument is fundamentally structural: when Δ_t ≤ 0 or below threshold, no parameter tuning helps. Varying τ would just show sensitivity analysis rather than reveal something new about the mechanism, so it's less compelling than the seed variance question.

The extended 1B run would be valuable but impractical—it'd take 3-4 days on a single GPU, which doesn't fit the timeline. So I'm weighing what actually moves the needle for EMNLP: the three-phase characterization, SLV as a monitoring tool, the signal attenuation paradox, and the phase diagram with r_crit are all solid contributions. Now I'm thinking through how a reviewer would score this—originality is strong with the paradox and phase diagram, but I need to assess whether the evidence is sufficient for a 4.0+ rating.

The soundness is the real constraint: single data domain, single architecture family, and some conditions tested with only one seed. Addressing domain or architecture would require completely new experimental pipelines that aren't feasible in four days, so multi-seed validation at the boundary conditions is the only realistic improvement I could pursue.

Looking at Table 3, the critical weak points are r=0.2 and r=0.5 being single-seed runs. The r=0.2 condition is particularly concerning because it captures that paradoxical finding where max Δ_t sits just below the τ=0.002 threshold—if that result doesn't replicate across seeds, the whole paradox claim needs qualification. Rather than chase more seeds, though, I think the better strategy is to strengthen the paper itself through tighter writing and clearer framing.

The experiment matrix is already solid for a characterization paper—five mixing ratios, multi-seed coverage at the critical boundary, seven alternative indicators, and cross-scale validation. Adding more seeds won't change the core story, just reduce noise in the table. EMNLP reviewers care deeply about clarity and presentation, so a polished paper with clean figures, correct numbers, and a compelling narrative will outperform one with an extra data point but sloppier execution. The real gains come from Phase E work: updating Tables 1 and 3 with the additional seed data I already have, fixing the Limitations section, correcting the "linear" claim, and running the SLV timing benchmark. That's high-impact, low-risk work. Any new experiment introduces uncertainty—if results contradict what I've found, I'm suddenly managing a much messier story.

If I had to pick one thing to run, the SLV timing benchmark is the priority since it directly validates the <1s versus ~70-minute claim that's currently unverified and takes only minutes to execute. For actual training experiments, an r=0.2 second seed would be most valuable because it sits at the edge of controller silence and would round out Table 3, but the risk-reward calculation is tighter there.

The timing is cutting it close though. Based on the previous experiment patterns, each generation cycle takes roughly 2 days on a single GPU when accounting for synthetic data generation, fine-tuning, and the downstream evaluation passes. If cuda:1 frees up around midnight on May 21, running the r=0.2 seed from May 22 would finish around May 24, which leaves almost no buffer before the deadline. I need to recalculate the per-generation timing more carefully to see if this is actually feasible.

But stepping back, I'm questioning whether this additional seed is the best use of time. The paper's experiment matrix is already solid, and one more r=0.2 seed probably only moves the needle by 0.1 points on reviewer soundness scores. Phase E polish—clean tables, verified numbers, polished writing, and a well-structured Limitations section—would likely have much higher impact on the final evaluation.

The F11 timing benchmark is also essentially free to run and directly supports a key claim, so that should take priority. Looking at potential reviewer concerns: the lack of statistical significance tests is acknowledged and acceptable for a characterization paper with error bars at key points; single domain and architecture limitations are honestly disclosed. None of these feel like reject-worthy gaps for EMNLP.

Now I should verify the experiment plan and coverage matrix to make sure nothing critical is missing.
```
